    inLanguage: 'fa-IR',

=== src/routes/quiz/[id]/+page.ts ===
import { error } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { CARTEX } from '#lib/data/cartex';

export const entries = () => CARTEX.map((_, i) => ({ id: String(i + 1) }));

export function load({ params }) {
  const index = Number(params.id);
  if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 1 || index > CARTEX.length) {
    error(404, 'آزمون مورد نظر پیدا نشد');
  }
  return { index, exam: CARTEX[index - 1], total: CARTEX.length };
}

=== src/routes/+page.svelte ===
<script lang="ts">
  import { browser } from '$app/env';
  import { resolve } from '$app/paths';
  import { page } from '$app/state';
  import Header from '#lib/components/Header.svelte';
  import Seo from '#lib/components/Seo.svelte';
  import StatusBadge from '#lib/components/StatusBadge.svelte';
  import { CARTEX } from '#lib/data/cartex';
  import { CONCEPT_COLORS, CONCEPT_SHAPES } from '#lib/data/concepts';
  import { LINES } from '#lib/data/lines';
  import { META } from '#lib/data/meta';
  import { SIGN_GRIDS } from '#lib/data/signgrids';
  import { SIGN_TYPES } from '#lib/data/signs';
  import { progress } from '#lib/stores/examProgress.svelte';
  import type { SignCategory } from '#lib/types';
  import { assetUrl } from '#lib/utils/asset';
  import { formatPercent, toPersianDigits } from '#lib/utils/formatters';

  const total = CARTEX.length;
  const questionTotal = CARTEX.reduce((sum, exam) => sum + exam.questions.length, 0);
  const signTotal = Object.values(SIGN_GRIDS).reduce((sum, items) => sum + items.length, 0);

  const stats = [
    { value: questionTotal, label: 'سوال اصلی' },
    { value: total, label: 'آزمون شبیه‌ساز' },
    { value: signTotal, label: 'تابلو با تصویر' },
    { value: LINES.length + CONCEPT_COLORS.length + CONCEPT_SHAPES.length, label: 'خط‌کشی و مفهوم' }
  ];

  const homeJsonLd = {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@graph': [
      {
        '@type': 'WebSite',
        name: 'آیین‌نامه رانندگی',
        url: 'https://ogpourya.github.io/ayin-nameh/',
        inLanguage: 'fa-IR',
        description: 'سوالات اصلی آیین‌نامه رانندگی به‌همراه آزمون‌های تستی شبیه‌ساز، برگه‌های اصلی، تابلوها و خط‌کشی‌ها.'
      },
      {
        '@type': 'Course',
        name: 'آمادگی آزمون آیین‌نامه رانندگی',
        description: 'سوالات اصلی آیین‌نامه رانندگی همراه با آزمون‌های شبیه‌ساز برای قبولی آسان در آزمون اصلی.',
        inLanguage: 'fa-IR',
        isAccessibleForFree: true,
        provider: { '@type': 'Organization', name: 'آیین‌نامه رانندگی', url: 'https://ogpourya.github.io/ayin-nameh/' },
        offers: { '@type': 'Offer', price: 0, priceCurrency: 'IRR', availability: 'https://schema.org/InStock' }
      }
    ]
  };

  const counts = $derived.by(() => {
    const passed = progress.passedCount;
    const failed = progress.failedCount;
    const inProgress = progress.inProgressCount;
    return { passed, failed, inProgress, notStarted: total - passed - failed - inProgress };
  });

  const readiness = $derived(Math.round((counts.passed / total) * 100));

  const signHref = (category: SignCategory) => {
    if (category.kind === 'concepts') return resolve('/concepts');
    if (category.kind === 'lines') return resolve('/lines');
    return resolve('/signs/[type]', { type: category.id });
  };

  // In-page anchors (#exams / #sheets / #signs) arrive either from the bottom nav or an in-page link.
  $effect(() => {
    const hash = page.url.hash;
    if (!browser || !hash) return;
    requestAnimationFrame(() =>
      document.querySelector(hash)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })
    );
  });
</script>

<Seo
  title="سوالات اصلی آیین‌نامه رانندگی + آزمون تستی رایگان"
  description={`${toPersianDigits(questionTotal)} سوال اصلی آیین‌نامه رانندگی در ${toPersianDigits(total)} آزمون شبیه‌ساز، همراه برگه‌های اصلی، تابلوها و خط‌کشی‌ها. کاملاً رایگان و بدون تبلیغات — بدون خواندن کل کتاب، در آزمون اصلی قبول شوید.`}
  jsonLd={homeJsonLd}
/>

<Header title={`${META.title} رانندگی`} back={false} />

<div class="relative">
  <img src={assetUrl('assets/img/header.webp')} alt="" class="h-40 w-full object-cover" />
  <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-900/80 via-slate-900/30 to-transparent"></div>
  <div class="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 p-4 text-white">
    <p class="text-lg font-black drop-shadow">{META.title} رانندگی</p>
    <p class="text-xs opacity-90">سوالات اصلی + آزمون شبیه‌ساز — کاملاً رایگان، بدون خواندن کل کتاب قبول شوید</p>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="space-y-8 p-4">
  <section class="fade-in grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
    {#each stats as stat (stat.label)}
      <div class="rounded-2xl border border-white/60 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 p-3 text-center shadow-sm backdrop-blur">
        <div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">{toPersianDigits(stat.value)}</div>
        <div class="mt-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{stat.label}</div>
      </div>
    {/each}
  </section>

  <section class="fade-in rounded-2xl border border-white/60 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 p-4 shadow-sm backdrop-blur">
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
      <h2 class="font-black">آمادگی شما</h2>
      <span class="rounded-full bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">
        {formatPercent(readiness)}
      </span>
    </div>
    <p class="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
      {toPersianDigits(counts.passed)} از {toPersianDigits(total)} آزمون قبول شدید
    </p>
    <div class="mt-3 h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700">
      <div
        class="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-l from-green-500 to-emerald-400 transition-all duration-500"
        style={`width: ${readiness}%`}
      ></div>
    </div>
    <div class="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-[11px] font-bold">
      <span class="rounded-full bg-green-100 dark:bg-green-950 px-2 py-1 text-green-700 dark:text-green-300">قبول {toPersianDigits(counts.passed)}</span>
      <span class="rounded-full bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-950 px-2 py-1 text-red-700 dark:text-red-300">مردود {toPersianDigits(counts.failed)}</span>
      <span class="rounded-full bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-900/70 px-2 py-1 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">
        در حال انجام {toPersianDigits(counts.inProgress)}
      </span>
      <span class="rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
        شروع نشده {toPersianDigits(counts.notStarted)}
      </span>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section id="sheets" class="scroll-mt-16">
    <h2 class="font-black">{META.sectionsTitle ?? 'برگه‌های اصلی آزمون'}</h2>
    <p class="mb-3 mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{META.help1} برگه‌های اصلی را مرور کنید و بعد آزمون بدهید.</p>
    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
      {#each CARTEX as exam, i (exam.title)}
        <a
          href={resolve('/pdf/[id]', { id: String(i + 1) })}
          class="card-click relative block overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/60 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 shadow-sm backdrop-blur hover:shadow-md"
        >
          {#if progress.visited[i + 1]}
            <span class="absolute left-2 top-2 z-10 rounded-full bg-indigo-600 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white">
              دیده شد
            </span>
          {/if}
          <img src={assetUrl(exam.cover)} alt={exam.title} loading="lazy" class="h-36 w-full bg-white object-cover object-top" />
          <div class="p-3 text-center">
            <div class="text-sm font-bold">{exam.title}</div>
            <div class="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{toPersianDigits(exam.questions.length)} سوال + برگه</div>
          </div>
        </a>
      {/each}
    </div>
  </section>

  <section id="exams" class="scroll-mt-16">
    <h2 class="font-black">{META.examsTitle ?? 'آزمون‌های تستی'}</h2>
    <p class="mb-3 mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
      حد نصاب قبولی: ۲۶ پاسخ درست و حداکثر ۴ پاسخ غلط.

    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
      {#each CARTEX as exam, i (exam.title)}
        {@const bookmarks = progress.bookmarkCountFor(i + 1)}
        <a
          href={resolve('/quiz/[id]', { id: String(i + 1) })}
          class="card-click block rounded-2xl border border-white/60 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 p-3 shadow-sm backdrop-blur hover:shadow-md"
        >
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div class="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-lg font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">
              {toPersianDigits(i + 1)}

          <div class="mt-3 text-sm font-bold">آزمون اصلی {toPersianDigits(i + 1)}</div>

  title={`${data.category.title} | تابلوهای رانندگی با تصویر و نام`}

    type="button"
    onclick={() => progress.setHideTexts(!progress.hideTexts)}
    aria-pressed={progress.hideTexts}
    class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700"
  >
    {#if progress.hideTexts}
      <Eye size={14} />
      نمایش متن
    {:else}
      <EyeOff size={14} />
      مخفی کردن متن
    {/if}
  </button>
</Header>

<div class="p-3">
  {#if progress.hideTexts}
    <p class="mb-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 px-3 py-2 text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">
      حالت سنجش حافظه فعال است — نام تابلوها پنهان شد
    </p>
  {/if}

  <div class="fade-in grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
    {#each data.items as item (item.img)}

=== src/routes/signs/[type]/+page.ts ===
import { error } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { SIGN_TYPES } from '#lib/data/signs';
import { SIGN_GRIDS } from '#lib/data/signgrids';

export const entries = () => SIGN_TYPES.filter((t) => t.kind === 'grid').map((t) => ({ type: t.id }));

export function load({ params }) {
  const category = SIGN_TYPES.find((t) => t.id === params.type);
  const items = category ? (SIGN_GRIDS[category.id] ?? []) : [];
  if (!category || category.kind !== 'grid' || items.length === 0) {
    error(404, 'دسته تابلو پیدا نشد');
  }
  return { category, items };
}

</script>

=== src/routes/pdf/[id]/+page.svelte ===
<script lang="ts">
  import { resolve } from '$app/paths';
  import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, X } from '@lucide/svelte';
  import Header from '#lib/components/Header.svelte';
  import Seo from '#lib/components/Seo.svelte';
  import { progress } from '#lib/stores/examProgress.svelte';
  import { assetUrl } from '#lib/utils/asset';
  import { toPersianDigits } from '#lib/utils/formatters';

  let { data } = $props();

  const pages = $derived(
    Array.from({ length: data.pageCount }, (_, i) => assetUrl(`assets/pages/exam${data.index}_-${i + 1}.jpg`))
  );

  let lightbox = $state<number | null>(null);

  $effect(() => {
    progress.markVisited(data.index);
  });

  function step(delta: number) {
    if (lightbox === null) return;
    lightbox = Math.min(Math.max(lightbox + delta, 0), pages.length - 1);
  }

  function onKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (lightbox === null) return;
    if (event.key === 'Escape') lightbox = null;
    else if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') step(1);
    else if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') step(-1);
  }
</script>

<Seo
  title={`${data.exam.title} | سوالات اصلی آیین‌نامه رانندگی با تصویر`}
  description={`مشاهده تصویری ${data.exam.title} به‌همراه ${toPersianDigits(data.exam.questions.length)} سوال اصلی آیین‌نامه رانندگی؛ آمادگی کاملاً رایگان برای آزمون اصلی.`}
/>

<svelte:window onkeydown={onKeydown} />

<Header title={data.exam.title} />

<div class="p-3">
  <div class="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between px-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
    <span>{toPersianDigits(pages.length)} صفحه</span>
    <a
      href={resolve('/quiz/[id]', { id: String(data.index) })}
      class="rounded-full bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-indigo-700 active:scale-95"
    >
      شروع آزمون {toPersianDigits(data.index)}
    </a>
  </div>

  <div class="fade-in space-y-3">
    {#each pages as page, i (page)}
      <button
        type="button"

      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>
</div>

=== src/routes/pdf/[id]/+page.ts ===
import { error } from '@sveltejs/kit';
import { CARTEX } from '#lib/data/cartex';
import { PAGE_COUNTS } from '#lib/data/pages';

export const entries = () => CARTEX.map((_, i) => ({ id: String(i + 1) }));

export function load({ params }) {
  const index = Number(params.id);
  if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 1 || index > CARTEX.length) {
    error(404, 'برگه مورد نظر پیدا نشد');
  }
  return { index, exam: CARTEX[index - 1], pageCount: PAGE_COUNTS[index] ?? 0 };
}

=== src/lib/utils/formatters.ts ===
const PERSIAN_DIGITS = ['۰', '۱', '۲', '۳', '۴', '۵', '۶', '۷', '۸', '۹'];

export const toPersianDigits = (value: number | string): string =>
  String(value).replace(/\d/g, (digit) => PERSIAN_DIGITS[Number(digit)]);

export const formatPercent = (value: number): string => `${toPersianDigits(Math.round(value))}٪`;

/** Seconds -> "m:ss" with Persian digits. */
export const formatDuration = (totalSeconds: number): string => {
  const safe = Math.max(0, Math.floor(totalSeconds));
  const minutes = Math.floor(safe / 60);
  const seconds = safe % 60;
  return toPersianDigits(`${minutes}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}`);
};

=== src/lib/utils/asset.ts ===
import { asset } from '$app/paths';
import type { AssetPath } from '$app/types';

/** Prefix a data-file asset path ("assets/img/x.png") with the deployment base path. */
export const assetUrl = (path: string): string => asset(path.replace(/^\/+/, '') as AssetPath);

=== src/lib/utils/exam.ts ===
/** Official cartex rule: pass with at least 26 correct answers and at most 4 mistakes. */
export const PASS_RULES = { minCorrect: 26, maxWrong: 4 } as const;

/** Short exams (fewer questions than the official 30) scale the correct-answer threshold down. */
export const minCorrectFor = (total: number): number =>
  Math.min(PASS_RULES.minCorrect, Math.max(1, total - PASS_RULES.maxWrong));

export const isPassed = (correct: number, wrong: number, total: number): boolean =>
  correct >= minCorrectFor(total) && wrong <= PASS_RULES.maxWrong;

=== src/lib/stores/examProgress.svelte.ts ===
import { browser } from '$app/env';
import { CARTEX } from '#lib/data/cartex';

/** Legacy-compatible status codes stored per exam. */
export const STATUS = { none: 0, passed: 1, failed: 2, inProgress: 3 } as const;

const STATUS_KEY = (exam: number) => `status_${exam}`;
const VISITED_KEY = (exam: number) => `exam${exam}`;
const BOOKMARKS_KEY = 'bookmarks';
const HIDE_TEXTS_KEY = 'hide_texts';

class ExamProgress {
  statuses = $state<Record<number, number>>({});
  visited = $state<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  bookmarks = $state<string[]>([]);
  hideTexts = $state(false);
  ready = $state(false);

  /** Load persisted state from localStorage. Client-only; call once on mount. */
  init() {
    if (!browser || this.ready) return;
    for (let exam = 1; exam <= CARTEX.length; exam += 1) {
      const status = Number(localStorage.getItem(STATUS_KEY(exam)) ?? 0);
      if (status) this.statuses[exam] = status;
      if (localStorage.getItem(VISITED_KEY(exam)) === 'true') this.visited[exam] = true;
    }
    try {
      const stored: unknown = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BOOKMARKS_KEY) ?? '[]');
      if (Array.isArray(stored)) {
        this.bookmarks = stored.filter((key): key is string => typeof key === 'string');
      }
    } catch {
      // Corrupted bookmarks: start clean, keep the rest of the state.
    }
    this.hideTexts = localStorage.getItem(HIDE_TEXTS_KEY) === '1';
    this.ready = true;
  }

  getStatus(exam: number): number {
    return this.statuses[exam] ?? STATUS.none;
  }

  setStatus(exam: number, status: number) {
    this.statuses[exam] = status;
    this.#write(STATUS_KEY(exam), String(status));
  }

  /** Mark an exam as started unless it was already passed or in progress. */
  startExam(exam: number) {
    const status = this.getStatus(exam);
    if (status !== STATUS.passed && status !== STATUS.inProgress) {
      this.setStatus(exam, STATUS.inProgress);
    }
  }

  markVisited(exam: number) {
    if (this.visited[exam]) return;
    this.visited[exam] = true;
    this.#write(VISITED_KEY(exam), 'true');
  }

  isBookmarked(exam: number, questionId: number): boolean {
    return this.bookmarks.includes(`${exam}-${questionId}`);
  }

  toggleBookmark(exam: number, questionId: number) {
    const key = `${exam}-${questionId}`;
    const at = this.bookmarks.indexOf(key);
    if (at >= 0) this.bookmarks.splice(at, 1);
    else this.bookmarks.push(key);
    this.#write(BOOKMARKS_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.bookmarks));
  }

  bookmarkCountFor(exam: number): number {
    return this.bookmarks.filter((key) => key.startsWith(`${exam}-`)).length;
  }

  setHideTexts(hidden: boolean) {
    this.hideTexts = hidden;
    this.#write(HIDE_TEXTS_KEY, hidden ? '1' : '0');
  }

  get passedCount(): number {
    return Object.values(this.statuses).filter((s) => s === STATUS.passed).length;
  }

  get failedCount(): number {
    return Object.values(this.statuses).filter((s) => s === STATUS.failed).length;
  }

  get inProgressCount(): number {
    return Object.values(this.statuses).filter((s) => s === STATUS.inProgress).length;
  }

  #write(key: string, value: string) {
    if (!browser) return;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(key, value);
    } catch {
      // Storage unavailable (private mode): the app keeps working in memory.
    }
  }
}

export const progress = new ExamProgress();

=== src/lib/components/Quiz.svelte ===
<script lang="ts">
  import { resolve } from '$app/paths';
  import { ArrowLeft, Bookmark, Check, Clock, ListChecks, RotateCcw, X } from '@lucide/svelte';
  import Header from '#lib/components/Header.svelte';
  import { STATUS, progress } from '#lib/stores/examProgress.svelte';
  import type { CartexExam, CartexQuestion } from '#lib/types';
  import { assetUrl } from '#lib/utils/asset';
  import { PASS_RULES, isPassed, minCorrectFor } from '#lib/utils/exam';
  import { formatDuration, formatPercent, toPersianDigits } from '#lib/utils/formatters';

  let { exam, index, total }: { exam: CartexExam; index: number; total: number } = $props();

  const questions = $derived(exam.questions);
  const passRule = $derived(minCorrectFor(questions.length));
  const persianKeyAlias: Record<string, string> = { '۱': '1', '۲': '2', '۳': '3', '۴': '4' };

  let mode = $state<'quiz' | 'result' | 'review'>('quiz');
  let current = $state(0);
  let picked = $state<number | null>(null);
  let answers = $state<Record<number, number>>({});
  let correctCount = $state(0);
  let wrongCount = $state(0);
  let elapsed = $state(0);
  let startedAt = Date.now();
  let reviewAt = $state(0);

  const question = $derived(questions[current]);
  const wrongList = $derived(
    Object.keys(answers)
      .map(Number)
      .filter((qi) => answers[qi] !== questions[qi].correctAnswer)
      .sort((a, b) => a - b)
  );
  const passed = $derived(isPassed(correctCount, wrongCount, questions.length));
  const percent = $derived(Math.round((correctCount / questions.length) * 100));
  const isBookmarked = $derived(progress.isBookmarked(index, question.id));

  $effect(() => {
    progress.startExam(index);
  });

  $effect(() => {
    if (mode !== 'quiz') return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000);
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  });

  function choose(choice: number) {
    if (picked !== null) return;
    picked = choice;
    answers[current] = choice;
    if (choice === question.correctAnswer) correctCount += 1;
    else wrongCount += 1;
  }

  function scrollTop() {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  }

  function next() {
    if (current + 1 >= questions.length) {
      progress.setStatus(index, passed ? STATUS.passed : STATUS.failed);
      mode = 'result';
    } else {
      current += 1;
      picked = null;
    }
    scrollTop();
  }

  function retry() {
    mode = 'quiz';
    current = 0;
    picked = null;
    answers = {};
    correctCount = 0;
    wrongCount = 0;
    elapsed = 0;
    startedAt = Date.now();
    scrollTop();
  }

  function startReview() {
    reviewAt = 0;
    mode = 'review';
    scrollTop();
  }

  function onKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.altKey) return;
    const key = persianKeyAlias[event.key] ?? event.key;
    if (mode === 'quiz') {
      if (picked === null) {
        const choice = Number(key);
        if (Number.isInteger(choice) && choice >= 1 && choice <= question.options.length) choose(choice);
      } else if (key === 'Enter') {
        next();
      }
    } else if (mode === 'result' && key === 'Enter') {
      retry();
    }
  }

  function optionClass(choice: number, correctAnswer: number, pickedChoice: number | null) {
    if (choice === correctAnswer) return 'border-green-500 bg-green-50';
    if (choice === pickedChoice) return 'border-red-500 bg-red-50';
    return 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 opacity-50';
  }

  function optionBadgeClass(choice: number, correctAnswer: number, pickedChoice: number | null) {
    if (choice === correctAnswer) return 'bg-green-600 text-white';
    if (choice === pickedChoice) return 'bg-red-600 text-white';
    return 'bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300';
  }
</script>

{#snippet optionList(q: CartexQuestion, pickedChoice: number | null, revealed: boolean)}
  <div class="mt-4 space-y-2">
    {#each q.options as option, idx (idx)}
      {@const choice = idx + 1}
      <button
        type="button"
        onclick={() => choose(choice)}
        disabled={revealed}
        class="flex min-h-12 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border-2 px-3 py-2 text-right transition
          {revealed
          ? optionClass(choice, q.correctAnswer, pickedChoice)
          : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:border-indigo-300 active:scale-[0.99]'}"
      >
        <span
          class="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg text-sm font-bold
            {revealed ? optionBadgeClass(choice, q.correctAnswer, pickedChoice) : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'}"
        >
          {toPersianDigits(choice)}
        </span>
        <span class="flex-1 leading-7">{option}</span>
        {#if revealed && choice === q.correctAnswer}
          <Check size={18} class="shrink-0 text-green-600" />
        {:else if revealed && choice === pickedChoice}
          <X size={18} class="shrink-0 text-red-600" />
        {/if}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>
{/snippet}

<svelte:window onkeydown={onKeydown} />

<Header
  title={`آزمون اصلی ${toPersianDigits(index)}`}
  subtitle={mode === 'quiz' ? `${toPersianDigits(current + 1)} / ${toPersianDigits(questions.length)}` : ''}
>
  <span class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
    <Clock size={14} />
    {formatDuration(elapsed)}
  </span>
</Header>

<div class="p-4">
  {#if mode === 'quiz'}
    <div class="mb-4 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700" role="progressbar" aria-valuenow={current + 1} aria-valuemin={1} aria-valuemax={questions.length}>
      <div
        class="h-full rounded-full bg-indigo-600 transition-all duration-300"
        style={`width: ${((current + 1) / questions.length) * 100}%`}
      ></div>
    </div>

    <div class="fade-in rounded-2xl border border-white/60 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 p-4 shadow-sm backdrop-blur">
      <div class="mb-2 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <span class="rounded-full bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">
          سوال {toPersianDigits(current + 1)}
        </span>
        <button
          type="button"
          onclick={() => progress.toggleBookmark(index, question.id)}
          aria-label="نشان‌کردن سوال برای مرور"
          aria-pressed={isBookmarked}
          class="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-full transition hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
        >
          <Bookmark size={18} class={isBookmarked ? 'fill-amber-400 text-amber-500' : 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'} />
        </button>
      </div>
      {#if question.image}
        <img
          src={assetUrl(question.image)}
          alt="تصویر سوال"
          loading="lazy"
          class="mx-auto mb-3 max-h-52 rounded-xl bg-white p-2 object-contain"
        />
      {/if}
      <p class="font-bold leading-7">{question.text}</p>
    </div>

    {@render optionList(question, picked, picked !== null)}

    {#if picked !== null}
      <button
        type="button"
        onclick={next}
        class="mt-4 flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-indigo-700 active:scale-[0.99]"
      >
        {current + 1 === questions.length ? 'دیدن نتیجه' : 'سوال بعدی'}
        <ArrowLeft size={18} />
      </button>
      <p class="mt-2 text-center text-[11px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
        میان‌بر: کلید ۱ تا ۴ برای انتخاب گزینه، Enter برای ادامه
      </p>
    {/if}
  {:else if mode === 'result'}
    <div class="fade-in py-6 text-center">
      <div class="text-5xl">{passed ? '🎉' : '😞'}</div>
      <div
        class="stamp mt-4 inline-block rounded-2xl border-4 px-6 py-3 text-2xl font-black
          {passed ? 'border-green-500 text-green-600' : 'border-red-500 text-red-600'}"
      >
        {passed ? 'قبول شدید!' : 'مردود شدید'}
      </div>

      <div class="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-sm space-y-2 rounded-2xl border border-white/60 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 p-4 text-sm leading-8 shadow-sm backdrop-blur">
        <div class="flex justify-between">
          <span>پاسخ درست</span>
          <b class="text-green-600">{toPersianDigits(correctCount)} از {toPersianDigits(questions.length)}</b>
        </div>
        <div class="flex justify-between">
          <span>پاسخ غلط</span>
          <b class="text-red-600">{toPersianDigits(wrongCount)}</b>
        </div>
        <div class="flex justify-between">
          <span>درصد</span>
          <b>{formatPercent(percent)}</b>
        </div>
        <div class="flex justify-between">
          <span>زمان</span>

        </div>
        <div class="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 pt-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          <span>حد نصاب</span>
          <span>{toPersianDigits(passRule)} درست و حداکثر {toPersianDigits(PASS_RULES.maxWrong)} غلط</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mx-auto mt-6 grid max-w-sm gap-3">
        {#if wrongList.length > 0}
          <button
            type="button"
            onclick={startReview}
            class="flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-amber-600 active:scale-[0.99]"
          >
            <ListChecks size={18} />
            مرور اشتباهات ({toPersianDigits(wrongList.length)})
          </button>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onclick={retry}
            class="flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-indigo-700 active:scale-[0.99]"
          >
            <RotateCcw size={18} />
            تلاش دوباره
          </button>
          <a
            href={resolve('/')}
            class="flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 py-3 font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 active:scale-[0.99]"
          >
            صفحه اصلی
          </a>
        </div>
        {#if index < total}
          <a
            href={resolve('/quiz/[id]', { id: String(index + 1) })}
            class="flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-green-600 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-green-700 active:scale-[0.99]"
          >
            آزمون بعدی {toPersianDigits(index + 1)}
            <ArrowLeft size={18} />
          </a>
        {/if}
      </div>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="fade-in">
      <div class="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span class="rounded-full bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
          مرور اشتباهات {toPersianDigits(reviewAt + 1)} از {toPersianDigits(wrongList.length)}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div class="rounded-2xl border border-white/60 dark:border-slate-700/60 bg-white/80 dark:bg-slate-900/70 p-4 shadow-sm backdrop-blur">
        {#if questions[wrongList[reviewAt]].image}
          <img
            src={assetUrl(questions[wrongList[reviewAt]].image as string)}
            alt="تصویر سوال"
            loading="lazy"
            class="mx-auto mb-3 max-h-52 rounded-xl bg-white p-2 object-contain"
          />
        {/if}
        <p class="font-bold leading-7">{questions[wrongList[reviewAt]].text}</p>
      </div>

      {@render optionList(questions[wrongList[reviewAt]], answers[wrongList[reviewAt]], true)}

      <div class="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={reviewAt === 0}
          onclick={() => (reviewAt -= 1)}
          class="min-h-12 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 py-3 font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          قبلی
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={reviewAt + 1 >= wrongList.length}
          onclick={() => (reviewAt += 1)}
          class="min-h-12 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 py-3 font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          بعدی
        </button>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        onclick={() => (mode = 'result')}
        class="mt-3 min-h-12 w-full rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-indigo-700 active:scale-[0.99]"
      >
        بازگشت به نتیجه
      </button>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>

=== src/lib/components/Seo.svelte ===
<script lang="ts">
  import { asset } from '$app/paths';
  import type { AssetPath } from '$app/types';
  import { page } from '$app/state';

  // Change when deploying to a custom domain.
  const SITE_ORIGIN = 'https://ogpourya.github.io';
  const SITE_NAME = 'آیین‌نامه رانندگی';
  const SHARE_IMAGE = SITE_ORIGIN + asset('assets/img/header.webp' as AssetPath);

  let {
    title,
    description,
    noindex = false,
    jsonLd = null
  }: {
    title: string;
    description: string;
    noindex?: boolean;
    jsonLd?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  } = $props();

  const canonical = $derived(SITE_ORIGIN + page.url.pathname);
  // Svelte does not evaluate expressions inside a template <script>, so build the tag as HTML.
  // The closing tag is escaped so it cannot terminate this component's own script block.
  const SCRIPT_CLOSE = '\x3c/script>';
  const jsonLdHtml = $derived(
    jsonLd
      ? `<script type="application/ld+json">${JSON.stringify(jsonLd).replace(/</g, '\\u003c')}${SCRIPT_CLOSE}`
      : ''
  );
</script>

<svelte:head>
  <title>{title}</title>
  <meta name="description" content={description} />
  <link rel="canonical" href={canonical} />
  {#if noindex}<meta name="robots" content="noindex" />{/if}
  <meta property="og:site_name" content={SITE_NAME} />
  <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
  <meta property="og:locale" content="fa_IR" />
  <meta property="og:title" content={title} />
  <meta property="og:description" content={description} />
  <meta property="og:url" content={canonical} />
  <meta property="og:image" content={SHARE_IMAGE} />
  <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
  <meta name="twitter:title" content={title} />
  <meta name="twitter:description" content={description} />
  <meta name="twitter:image" content={SHARE_IMAGE} />
</svelte:head>

{@html jsonLdHtml}

=== src/lib/components/BottomNav.svelte ===
<script lang="ts">
  import { page } from '$app/state';
  import { resolve } from '$app/paths';
  import { ClipboardList, FileImage, Home, LayoutGrid } from '@lucide/svelte';

  const items = [
    { label: 'خانه', href: resolve('/'), icon: Home },
    { label: 'برگه‌ها', href: resolve('/#sheets'), icon: FileImage },
    { label: 'آزمون‌ها', href: resolve('/#exams'), icon: ClipboardList },
    { label: 'تابلوها', href: resolve('/#signs'), icon: LayoutGrid }
  ];

  const isActive = (href: string) => `${page.url.pathname}${page.url.hash}` === href;
</script>

<nav
  class="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/70 bg-white/85 dark:bg-slate-900/85 pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)] backdrop-blur-xl"
  aria-label="ناوبری اصلی"
>
  <div class="mx-auto grid max-w-3xl grid-cols-4">
    {#each items as item (item.label)}
      <a
        href={item.href}
        class="flex min-h-14 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition
          {isActive(item.href) ? 'text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-800'}"
      >
        <item.icon size={20} />
        <span>{item.label}</span>
      </a>
    {/each}
  </div>
</nav>

  let { status }: { status: number } = $props();
</script>

{#if status === STATUS.passed}
  <span class="rounded-full bg-green-100 dark:bg-green-950 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-green-700 dark:text-green-300">قبول شده</span>
{:else if status === STATUS.failed}
  <span class="rounded-full bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-950 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-red-700 dark:text-red-300">مردود</span>
{:else if status === STATUS.inProgress}
  <span class="rounded-full bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-900/70 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">در حال انجام</span>
{/if}

معمولاً به رنگ زرد هستند و به صورت زیگزاگی در حاشیه راه‌های متصل به پیاده‌روها ترسیم می‌شوند و عبور کردن و ایستادن روی آنها ممنوع می‌باشد.\n\nخطوط پارک: جهت پارک وسایل نقلیه از این خطوط استفاده می‌شود که در کنار جداول خیابان‌ها رسم می‌شوند و سه نوع آن عبارتند از خطوط پارک افقی، خطوط پارک مورب و خطوط پارک عمودی.\n\nدیگری از خط‌کشی‌ها خط‌کشی‌های عرضی هستند که در عرض معابر رسم می‌شوند و مهم‌ترین آنها عبارتند از خط‌کشی‌های عابر پیاده، خط ممتد ایست و خطوط توقف منقطع، که در ادامه تعریف مختصری از هر یک از آنها ارائه کرده‌ایم:\n\nخط کشی عبور پیاده: این خط‌کشی‌ها برای عبور عابرین پیاده از عرض خیابان‌ها استفاده می‌شود و در ورودی تقاطع یا محل‌های معینی از معابر رسم می‌شوند.\n\nخط ممتد ایست: رانندگانی که به این خطوط می‌رسند، باید قبل از خطوط ممتده ایست، که معمولاً در تقاطع‌ها رسم می‌شود، به صورت کامل === scripts/convert-data.mjs ===
// Converts the legacy vanilla-JS data globals (window.CARTEX, ...) into typed TS modules.
// Usage: npm run convert:data   (override source dir with SRC=/path/to/driving)
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import vm from 'node:vm';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const SRC = process.env.SRC ?? '/home/ogpourya/Downloads/driving';
const OUT = join(ROOT, 'src', 'lib', 'data');

const FILES = ['meta.js', 'cartex.js', 'signs.js', 'signgrids.js', 'concepts.js', 'lines.js'];

const data = {};
for (const file of FILES) {
  const context = vm.createContext({ window: {} });
  vm.runInContext(readFileSync(join(SRC, 'data', file), 'utf8'), context, { filename: file });
  Object.assign(data, context.window);
}

const { META, CARTEX, SIGN_TYPES, SIGN_GRIDS, CONCEPTS, LINES } = data;

const banner = '// Generated by scripts/convert-data.mjs from the legacy data files. Regenerate with: npm run convert:data\n\n';
const emit = (file, body) => {
  mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(join(OUT, file), banner + body);
};

emit(
  'meta.ts',
  `import type { MetaInfo } from '../types';\n\nexport const META: MetaInfo = ${JSON.stringify(META)};\n`
);
emit(
  'cartex.ts',
  `import type { CartexExam } from '../types';\n\nexport const CARTEX: CartexExam[] = ${JSON.stringify(CARTEX)};\n`
);
emit(
  'signs.ts',
  `import type { SignCategory } from '../types';\n\nexport const SIGN_TYPES: SignCategory[] = ${JSON.stringify(SIGN_TYPES)};\n`
);
emit(
  'signgrids.ts',
  `import type { SignGridItem } from '../types';\n\nexport const SIGN_GRIDS: Record<string, SignGridItem[]> = ${JSON.stringify(SIGN_GRIDS)};\n`
);
emit(
  'concepts.ts',
  `import type { ConceptColor, ConceptShape } from '../types';\n\nexport const CONCEPT_COLORS: ConceptColor[] = ${JSON.stringify(CONCEPTS.colors)};\n\nexport const CONCEPT_SHAPES: ConceptShape[] = ${JSON.stringify(CONCEPTS.shapes)};\n`
);
emit(
  'lines.ts',
  `import type { LineItem } from '../types';\n\nexport const LINES: LineItem[] = ${JSON.stringify(LINES)};\n`
);

// Exam sheet scans: examN_-P.jpg -> how many pages each exam has.
const pageCounts = {};
for (const name of readdirSync(join(SRC, 'assets', 'pages'))) {
  const match = name.match(/^exam(\d+)_-(\d+)\./);
  if (match) pageCounts[Number(match[1])] = Math.max(pageCounts[Number(match[1])] ?? 0, Number(match[2]));
}
const sortedPageCounts = Object.fromEntries(
  Object.keys(pageCounts)
    .map(Number)
    .sort((a, b) => a - b)
    .map((n) => [n, pageCounts[n]])
);
emit(
  'pages.ts',
  `/** Number of sheet-scan images available per exam id (page images live under static/assets/pages). */\nexport const PAGE_COUNTS: Record<number, number> = ${JSON.stringify(sortedPageCounts)};\n`
);

// ---- data validation report ----
const problems = [];
CARTEX.forEach((exam, i) => {
  const n = i + 1;
  if (!exam.questions.length) problems.push(`exam ${n}: no questions`);
  for (const q of exam.questions) {
    if (!q.text) problems.push(`exam ${n} q${q.id}: empty text`);
    if (q.options.length !== 4) problems.push(`exam ${n} q${q.id}: ${q.options.length} options`);
    if (!Number.isInteger(q.correctAnswer) || q.correctAnswer < 1 || q.correctAnswer > q.options.length) {
      problems.push(`exam ${n} q${q.id}: bad correctAnswer ${q.correctAnswer}`);
    }
  }
  if (!pageCounts[n]) problems.push(`exam ${n}: no sheet pages on disk`);
});

const perExam = CARTEX.map((exam) => exam.questions.length);
console.log(`exams: ${CARTEX.length}`);
console.log(`questions: ${perExam.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)} (min ${Math.min(...perExam)}, max ${Math.max(...perExam)})`);
console.log(
  `sign categories: ${SIGN_TYPES.length}, grids: ${Object.keys(SIGN_GRIDS).length}, colors: ${CONCEPTS.colors.length}, shapes: ${CONCEPTS.shapes.length}, lines: ${LINES.length}`
);
if (problems.length) {
  console.log(`\nproblems (${problems.length}):`);
  for (const problem of problems.slice(0, 40)) console.log(`- ${problem}`);
} else {
  console.log('data checks: all good');
}

=== scripts/generate-sitemap.mjs ===
// Generates static/sitemap.xml for the deployed site. Runs before vite build.
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { CARTEX } from '../src/lib/data/cartex.ts';
import { SIGN_TYPES } from '../src/lib/data/signs.ts';

const ORIGIN = 'https://ogpourya.github.io';
const BASE = '/ayin-nameh';
const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

const entries = [
  { path: '/', priority: '1.0' },
  { path: '/concepts', priority: '0.7' },
  { path: '/lines', priority: '0.7' },
  ...CARTEX.map((_, i) => ({ path: `/quiz/${i + 1}`, priority: '0.9' })),
  ...CARTEX.map((_, i) => ({ path: `/pdf/${i + 1}`, priority: '0.8' })),
  ...SIGN_TYPES.filter((type) => type.kind === 'grid').map((type) => ({
    path: `/signs/${type.id}`,
    priority: '0.7'
  }))
];

const xml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">
${entries
  .map(
    (entry) => `  <url>
    <loc>${ORIGIN}${BASE}${entry.path}</loc>
    <lastmod>${today}</lastmod>
    <priority>${entry.priority}</priority>
  </url>`
  )
  .join('\n')}
</urlset>
`;

writeFileSync(new URL('../static/sitemap.xml', import.meta.url), xml);
console.log(`sitemap: ${entries.length} urls`);
